Code:

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""
Duplicate image finder v2 — for massive file counts
=================================================

Features:
  * Menu: [1] Start/continue searching (resume from checkpoint)  [2] Rescan  [3] Export list (CSV+Markdown)
  * SQLite cache: interruption-resumable, incremental scan (skips files with unchanged mtime/size)
  * Tiered funnel: filename -> size -> file head hash -> full-file hash -> (optional) perceptual hash visual similarity
  * Multiprocessing hashing; real-time on-screen output of duplicate groups as discovered

Duplicate types:
  * Filename duplicates: same basename (case-insensitive)
  * Content duplicates: byte-level identical (BLAKE2b-128 full file hash)
  * Visual similarity (optional): pHash 64-bit fingerprint + BK-tree + Hamming distance clustering,
    can recognize re-compressed / resolution-changed / watermarked copies

Dependencies: pip install pillow imagehash tqdm
Usage:
  python duplicate_image_finder_v2.py                # interactive menu
  python duplicate_image_finder_v2.py scan <dir> [--fresh] [--no-visual] [--threshold 6] [--workers 8]
  python duplicate_image_finder_v2.py export
"""
```

Constants:
```python
IMAGE_EXTS = {'.jpg','.jpeg','.png','.bmp','.gif','.webp','.tiff','.tif'}
DB_FILENAME = 'image_dedup.db'
PREFIX_BYTES = 64*1024
CHUNK_SIZE = 2000          # files per worker task
BATCH = 5000               # DB batch size
HASH_SIZE = 8
DEFAULT_THRESHOLD = 6
```

- tqdm around ex.map results.

Enumeration function returns (total, new_or_changed, deleted, skipped_placeholder, errors). upsert's changes detection: cursor.rowcount on executemany is unreliable... for counting changed: run per-batch `SELECT COUNT(*) ...` to count? Simplification: rely on counting in the upsert: changes = 0; per row in batch: hmm 650k individual statements are slow.

Alternative approach for change detection:
Before upsert, fetch existing (path→(size,mtime,name)) for batch paths via SELECT; compare in Python; split batch into inserts / updates (changed) / untouched. Count and upsert only what's needed. Deterministic and cheap: SELECT id,path,size,mtime,name FROM files WHERE path IN (...). Per batch of 5000 — fine.

Then:
- INSERT OR IGNORE for new paths (or plain INSERT)
- UPDATE for changed (reset hashes), count them
- Untouched: nothing
Collect ids for seen.

This gives clean counters.

Exact-match duplicate live display: load known map before stage (files that already have full_hash). During chunk processing, update + display. This displays new groups for the first time. At end of stage also compute stats via SQL.

Display function:

```python
def show_group(kind, name, paths, extra=None):
    lines = [f"--{name}  [{kind}]"]
    lines.extend(paths)
    tqdm.write("\n".join(lines) + "\n")
```
User's format: filename line, then paths. Extra marker for additions:
`+ {path}  (added to group: {name})`

If first time visual → changed doesn't matter, cluster anyway. Condition: if this run's changed==0 and no new phash computed and conn has some visual_group or meta 'visual_done' → skip. Meta keys set: root, visual_done=1 after clustering. Skip condition: changed==0 and newly_phashed==0 and meta visual_done=='1' and phash IS NULL among reps == 0. Implement carefully.

Actually simpler and more robust: run clustering whenever (changed + newly_phashed) > 0 or meta visual_done != '1'. Skip otherwise. ✓

visual_group global clear only when clustering runs.

Also don't forget Windows multiprocessing freeze_support — `if __name__ == '__main__': main()` is enough for spawn? Windows needs freeze_support only for frozen executables. Fine.

Also for worker pool: use with block; on KeyboardInterrupt during ex.map — executor terminates; acceptable.

One more performance point: SELECT ... ORDER BY full_hash on 650k rows uses index ix_full ✓.

{
  "content": "#!/usr/bin/env python3\n# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-\n\"\"\"\n重复图片查找工具 v2 —— 面向海量文件（十万~百万级）\n=====================================================\n\n特性:\n  * 交互菜单: [1] 开始/继续查找(断点续扫)  [2] 重新扫描  [3] 导出列表(CSV+Markdown)\n  * SQLite 缓存: 中断后可续扫; 增量扫描自动跳过 大小+mtime 未变化的文件\n  * 分级漏斗加速: 文件名 -> 文件大小 -> 文件头哈希 -> 整文件哈希 -> (可选)感知哈希\n  * 多进程并行计算哈希; 查找过程中实时打印已发现的重复组\n\n重复类型:\n  * 文件名重复: 基础文件名相同(不区分大小写), 与内容无关\n  * 内容重复:   字节级完全一致(整文件 BLAKE2b-128 哈希)\n  * 视觉相似(可选): pHash 64位指纹 + BK树 + 汉明距离聚类,\n    可识别重新压缩/改分辨率/加水印/改格式后肉眼相同的图片\n\n依赖:\n  pip install pillow imagehash tqdm\n\n用法:\n  python duplicate_image_finder_v2.py                          # 交互菜单\n  python duplicate_image_finder_v2.py scan <目录>              # 续扫\n  python duplicate_image_finder_v2.py scan <目录> --fresh      # 重新扫描\n  python duplicate_image_finder_v2.py scan <目录> --no-visual  # 只查字节级重复\n  python duplicate_image_finder_v2.py export                   # 导出报告\n\"\"\"\n\nimport argparse\nimport hashlib\nimport os\nimport sqlite3\nimport sys\nimport time\nfrom collections import defaultdict\nfrom concurrent.futures import ProcessPoolExecutor\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nfrom tqdm import tqdm\n\n# ----------------------------- 常量 -----------------------------\n\nIMAGE_EXTS = {'.jpg', '.jpeg', '.png', '.bmp', '.gif', '.webp', '.tiff', '.tif'}\nDB_FILENAME = 'image_dedup.db'\nPREFIX_BYTES = 64 * 1024      # 文件头哈希的采样长度\nCHUNK_SIZE = 2000             # 每个进程任务处理的文件数\nBATCH = 5000                  # 数据库批量提交大小\nHASH_SIZE = 8                 # pHash 8x8 = 64 位指纹\nDEFAULT_THRESHOLD = 6         # 汉明距离阈值(0=完全一致, 建议 5~8)\n\nKIND_NAME = '文件名重复'\nKIND_EXACT = '内容重复'\nKIND_VISUAL = '视觉相似'\n\n# ------------------- Windows OneDrive 占位文件检测 -------------------\n\nif sys.platform == 'win32':\n    import ctypes\n    from ctypes import wintypes\n\n    _k32 = ctypes.WinDLL('kernel32', use_last_error=True)\n    _k32.GetFileAttributesW.argtypes = [wintypes.LPCWSTR]\n    _k32.GetFileAttributesW.restype = wintypes.DWORD\n    _F_OFFLINE = 0x1000\n    _F_RECALL_ON_OPEN = 0x40000\n    _F_RECALL_ON_DATA = 0x400000\n\n    def is_placeholder(path: str) -> bool:\n        \"\"\"OneDrive 按需文件: 读取内容会触发云端下载, 扫描时跳过\"\"\"\n        p = '\\\\\\\\?\\\\' + os.path.abspath(path) if len(path) >= 250 else path\n        attrs = _k32.GetFileAttributesW(p)\n        return attrs != 0xFFFFFFFF and bool(attrs & (_F_OFFLINE | _F_RECALL_ON_OPEN | _F_RECALL_ON_DATA))\nelse:\n    def is_placeholder(path: str) -> bool:\n        return False\n\n# ----------------------------- 数据库 -----------------------------\n\nSCHEMA = \"\"\"\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS files(\n    id          INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,\n    path        TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,\n    name        TEXT NOT NULL,\n    size        INTEGER NOT NULL,\n    mtime       REAL NOT NULL,\n    prefix_hash TEXT,\n    full_hash   TEXT,\n    phash       TEXT,\n    visual_group INTEGER,\n    error       TEXT\n);\nCREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS ix_files_size ON files(size);\nCREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS ix_files_name ON files(name COLLATE NOCASE);\nCREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS ix_files_full ON files(full_hash);\nCREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS ix_files_vg   ON files(visual_group);\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS meta(key TEXT PRIMARY KEY, value TEXT);\n\"\"\"\n\n\ndef open_db(db_path: str) -> sqlite3.Connection:\n    conn = sqlite3.connect(db_path)\n    conn.execute('PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL')\n    conn.execute('PRAGMA synchronous=NORMAL')\n    conn.executescript(SCHEMA)\n    return conn\n\n\ndef get_meta(conn, key, default=None):\n    row = conn.execute('SELECT value FROM meta WHERE key=?', (key,)).fetchone()\n    return row[0] if row else default\n\n\ndef set_meta(conn, key, value):\n    conn.execute('INSERT INTO meta(key,value) VALUES(?,?) '\n                 'ON CONFLICT(key) DO UPDATE SET value=excluded.value', (key, str(value)))\n\n\ndef db_file_count(conn):\n    return conn.execute('SELECT COUNT(*) FROM files').fetchone()[0]\n\n# ----------------------------- 目录遍历 -----------------------------\n\n\ndef walk_images(root: str):\n    \"\"\"确定性(按名称排序)递归遍历, 产出图片文件路径\"\"\"\n    stack = [root]\n    while stack:\n        d = stack.pop()\n        try:\n            entries = sorted(os.scandir(d), key=lambda e: e.name.lower())\n        except OSError as e:\n            tqdm.write(f'[警告] 无法进入目录 {d}: {e}')\n            continue\n        for entry in entries:\n            try:\n                if entry.is_dir(follow_symlinks=False):\n                    stack.append(entry.path)\n                elif entry.is_file(follow_symlinks=False):\n                    if os.path.splitext(entry.name)[1].lower() in IMAGE_EXTS:\n                        yield entry.path\n            except OSError:\n                continue\n\n\ndef enumerate_into_db(conn, root: str, db_abs: str):\n    \"\"\"遍历目录并同步到数据库。返回 (总数, 新增, 变化, 删除, 占位跳过, 出错)\"\"\"\n    added = changed = deleted = skipped_ph = stat_err = 0\n    seen = set()\n    batch = []\n\n    def flush():\n        nonlocal added, changed\n        if not batch:\n            return\n        paths = [b[0] for b in batch]\n        qmarks = ','.join('?' * len(paths))\n        existing = {r[1]: r for r in conn.execute(\n            f'SELECT id,path,size,mtime,name FROM files WHERE path IN ({qmarks})', paths)}\n        inserts, updates = [], []\n        for path, name, size, mtime in batch:\n            row = existing.get(path)\n            if row is None:\n                inserts.append((path, name, size, mtime))\n                added += 1\n            elif row[2] != size or row[3] != mtime or row[4] != name:\n                updates.append((size, mtime, name, row[0]))\n                changed += 1\n            seen.add(row[0] if row else None)\n        if inserts:\n            conn.executemany('INSERT INTO files(path,name,size,mtime) VALUES(?,?,?,?)', inserts)\n        if updates:\n            conn.executemany(\n                'UPDATE files SET size=?, mtime=?, name=?, prefix_hash=NULL, full_hash=NULL, '\n                'phash=NULL, visual_group=NULL, error=NULL WHERE id=?', updates)\n        if inserts:\n            new_paths = [i[0] for i in inserts]\n            qm2 = ','.join('?' * len(new_paths))\n            for (fid,) in conn.execute(f'SELECT id FROM files WHERE path IN ({qm2})', new_paths):\n                seen.add(fid)\n        seen.discard(None)\n        conn.commit()\n        batch.clear()\n\n    for path in walk_images(root):\n        if os.path.abspath(path) == db_abs:\n            continue\n        if is_placeholder(path):\n            skipped_ph += 1\n            continue\n        try:\n            st = os.stat(path)\n        except OSError:\n            stat_err += 1\n            continue\n        batch.append((path, os.path.basename(path), st.st_size, st.st_mtime))\n        if len(batch) >= BATCH:\n            flush()\n    flush()\n\n    # 删除已不存在的文件记录\n    conn.execute('CREATE TEMP TABLE seen_ids(id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY)')\n    ids = sorted(seen)\n    for i in range(0, len(ids), BATCH):\n        conn.executemany('INSERT OR IGNORE INTO seen_ids(id) VALUES(?)',\n                         [(x,) for x in ids[i:i + BATCH]])\n    deleted = conn.execute('DELETE FROM files WHERE id NOT IN (SELECT id FROM seen_ids)').rowcount\n    conn.execute('DROP TABLE seen_ids')\n    conn.commit()\n\n    total = db_file_count(conn)\n    return total, added, changed, deleted or 0, skipped_ph, stat_err\n\n# ----------------------------- 工作进程 -----------------------------\n\n\ndef hash_chunk(task):\n    \"\"\"计算文件哈希。task = (mode, [(id, path), ...]); mode: 'prefix'|'full'\"\"\"\n    mode, jobs = task\n    out = []\n    for fid, path in jobs:\n        try:\n            h = hashlib.blake2b(digest_size=16)\n            with open(path, 'rb') as f:\n                if mode == 'prefix':\n                    h.update(f.read(PREFIX_BYTES))\n                else:\n                    while True:\n                        block = f.read(1 << 20)\n                        if not block:\n                            break\n                        h.update(block)\n            out.append((fid, h.hexdigest(), None))\n        except Exception as e:\n            out.append((fid, None, f'{type(e).__name__}: {e}'[:200]))\n    return out\n\n\ndef phash_chunk(jobs):\n    \"\"\"计算感知哈希。jobs = [(id, path), ...]\"\"\"\n    import imagehash\n    from PIL import Image\n    out = []\n    for fid, path in jobs:\n        try:\n            with Image.open(path) as im:\n                try:\n                    im.draft('L', (64, 64))  # JPEG 快速降采样, 显著提速\n                except Exception:\n                    pass\n                hv = imagehash.phash(im, hash_size=HASH_SIZE)\n            out.append((fid, str(hv), None))\n        except Exception as e:\n            out.append((fid, None, f'{type(e).__name__}: {e}'[:200]))\n    return out\n\n# ----------------------------- 哈希阶段 -----------------------------\n\n\ndef _run_stage(conn, sql, worker_fn, mode, workers, desc):\n    \"\"\"通用并行哈希阶段: 按 sql 选出 (id, path), 并行计算, 批量入库。\n    返回 (处理数, 失败数, 结果迭代期间的回调由调用方处理)。\"\"\"\n    rows = conn.execute(sql).fetchall()\n    if not rows:\n        return 0, 0, []\n\n    chunks = [rows[i:i + CHUNK_SIZE] for i in range(0, len(rows), CHUNK_SIZE)]\n    tasks = [(mode, c) for c in chunks] if mode else chunks\n    failures, results = [], []\n    col = {'prefix': 'prefix_hash', 'full': 'full_hash', None: 'phash'}[mode]\n\n    with ProcessPoolExecutor(max_workers=workers) as ex, \\\n            tqdm(total=len(rows), desc=desc, unit='文件') as bar:\n        for chunk_res in ex.map(worker_fn, tasks):\n            updates, errs = [], []\n            for fid, digest, err in chunk_res:\n                results.append((fid, digest, err))\n                if digest is not None:\n                    updates.append((digest, fid))\n                else:\n                    errs.append((err, fid))\n                    failures.append((fid, err))\n            if updates:\n                conn.executemany(f'UPDATE files SET {col}=? WHERE id=?', updates)\n            if errs:\n                conn.executemany('UPDATE files SET error=? WHERE id=?', errs)\n            conn.commit()\n            bar.update(len(chunk_res))\n    return len(rows), len(failures), results\n\n\ndef stage_prefix_hash(conn, workers):\n    conn.execute('DROP TABLE IF EXISTS dup_sizes')\n    conn.execute('CREATE TEMP TABLE dup_sizes AS '\n                 'SELECT size FROM files GROUP BY size HAVING COUNT(*)>1')\n    n, fails, _ = _run_stage(\n        conn,\n        'SELECT f.id, f.path FROM files f JOIN dup_sizes d ON f.size=d.size '\n        'WHERE f.prefix_hash IS NULL AND f.error IS NULL',\n        hash_chunk, 'prefix', workers, '文件头哈希(仅大小重复的文件)')\n    return n, fails\n\n\ndef stage_full_hash(conn, workers, live_print=True):\n    conn.execute('DROP TABLE IF EXISTS dup_prefix')\n    conn.execute('CREATE TEMP TABLE dup_prefix AS '\n                 'SELECT size, prefix_hash FROM files WHERE prefix_hash IS NOT NULL '\n                 'GROUP BY size, prefix_hash HAVING COUNT(*)>1')\n\n    # 已有整文件哈希的组(用于实时发现新成员)\n    known = defaultdict(list)\n    for h, path in conn.execute(\n            'SELECT full_hash, path FROM files WHERE full_hash IS NOT NULL ORDER BY id'):\n        known[h].append(path)\n\n    rows = conn.execute(\n        'SELECT f.id, f.path FROM files f JOIN dup_prefix d '\n        'ON f.size=d.size AND f.prefix_hash=d.prefix_hash '\n        'WHERE f.full_hash IS NULL AND f.error IS NULL').fetchall()\n    if not rows:\n        return 0, 0\n\n    chunks = [rows[i:i + CHUNK_SIZE] for i in range(0, len(rows), CHUNK_SIZE)]\n    failures = 0\n    with ProcessPoolExecutor(max_workers=workers) as ex, \\\n            tqdm(total=len(rows), desc='整文件哈希(仅头部冲突的文件)', unit='文件') as bar:\n        for chunk_res in ex.map(hash_chunk, [('full', c) for c in chunks]):\n            updates, errs = [], []\n            for fid, digest, err in chunk_res:\n                if digest is not None:\n                    updates.append((digest, fid))\n                else:\n                    errs.append((err, fid))\n                    failures += 1\n            if updates:\n                conn.executemany('UPDATE files SET full_hash=? WHERE id=?', updates)\n            if errs:\n                conn.executemany('UPDATE files SET error=? WHERE id=?', errs)\n            conn.commit()\n\n            if live_print:\n                qm = ','.join('?' * len(updates))\n                if updates:\n                    for h, path in conn.execute(\n                            f'SELECT full_hash, path FROM files WHERE id IN ({qm}) ORDER BY id',\n                            [u[1] for u in updates]):\n                        lst = known[h]\n                        lst.append(path)\n                        if len(lst) == 2:\n                            show_group(KIND_EXACT, os.path.basename(lst[0]), lst)\n                        elif len(lst) > 2:\n                            tqdm.write(f'+ 同组新增: {path}  (组: {os.path.basename(lst[0])})\\n')\n            bar.update(len(chunk_res))\n    conn.commit()\n    return len(rows), failures\n\n# ----------------------------- BK 树聚类 -----------------------------\n\n\nclass _BKNode:\n    __slots__ = ('h', 'idxs', 'children')\n\n    def __init__(self, h, idx):\n        self.h = h\n        self.idxs = [idx]\n        self.children = {}\n\n\nclass BKTree:\n    \"\"\"迭代式 BK 树, 按汉明距离近邻查询; 相同哈希合并到一个节点\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self):\n        self.root = None\n\n    def insert(self, h, idx):\n        if self.root is None:\n            self.root = _BKNode(h, idx)\n            return\n        cur = self.root\n        while True:\n            d = (h ^ cur.h).bit_count()\n            if d == 0:\n                cur.idxs.append(idx)\n                return\n            nxt = cur.children.get(d)\n            if nxt is None:\n                cur.children[d] = _BKNode(h, idx)\n                return\n            cur = nxt\n\n    def query(self, h, threshold, out):\n        stack = [self.root]\n        while stack:\n            node = stack.pop()\n            d = (h ^ node.h).bit_count()\n            if d <= threshold:\n                out.extend(node.idxs)\n            lo, hi = d - threshold, d + threshold\n            for dd, child in node.children.items():\n                if lo <= dd <= hi:\n                    stack.append(child)\n\n\nclass UnionFind:\n    def __init__(self, n):\n        self.parent = list(range(n))\n        self.rank = [0] * n\n\n    def find(self, x):\n        while self.parent[x] != x:\n            self.parent[x] = self.parent[self.parent[x]]\n            x = self.parent[x]\n        return x\n\n    def union(self, a, b):\n        ra, rb = self.find(a), self.find(b)\n        if ra == rb:\n            return\n        if self.rank[ra] < self.rank[rb]:\n            ra, rb = rb, ra\n        self.parent[rb] = ra\n        if self.rank[ra] == self.rank[rb]:\n            self.rank[ra] += 1\n\n\ndef stage_visual(conn, workers, threshold, skip_if_clean):\n    \"\"\"视觉相似检测: 对代表文件算 pHash, BK树聚类, 展开成组。\n    返回 (新算哈希数, 组数)。\"\"\"\n    # 代表 = 每个整文件哈希组取第一个 + 所有无整文件哈希的文件(大小唯一/头部唯一)\n    conn.execute('DROP TABLE IF EXISTS reps')\n    conn.execute(\n        'CREATE TEMP TABLE reps AS '\n        'SELECT MIN(id) AS id FROM files WHERE full_hash IS NOT NULL GROUP BY full_hash '\n        'UNION '\n        'SELECT id FROM files WHERE full_hash IS NULL AND error IS NULL')\n\n    n_new, fails, _ = _run_stage(\n        conn,\n        'SELECT r.id, f.path FROM reps r JOIN files f ON f.id=r.id WHERE f.phash IS NULL',\n        phash_chunk, None, workers, '感知哈希 pHash(视觉相似)')\n    if fails:\n        tqdm.write(f'[提示] {fails} 张图片无法解码, 已跳过视觉相似检测')\n\n    rep_rows = conn.execute(\n        'SELECT r.id, f.phash, f.full_hash FROM reps r JOIN files f ON f.id=r.id '\n        'WHERE f.phash IS NOT NULL ORDER BY r.id').fetchall()\n\n    visual_done_before = get_meta(conn, 'visual_done') == '1'\n    if skip_if_clean and n_new == 0 and visual_done_before:\n        tqdm.write('缓存无变化, 沿用上次视觉相似分组 (如需强制重算请选 2 重新扫描)')\n        return 0, conn.execute(\n            'SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT visual_group) FROM files WHERE visual_group IS NOT NULL').fetchone()[0]\n\n    conn.execute('UPDATE files SET visual_group=NULL')\n    conn.commit()\n\n    hashes = [int(h, 16) for _, h, _ in rep_rows]\n    n = len(hashes)\n    uf = UnionFind(n)\n    tree = BKTree()\n    hits_buf = []\n    for i in tqdm(range(n), desc='BK树聚类(汉明距离)', unit='哈希'):\n        hits_buf.clear()\n        tree.query(hashes[i], threshold, hits_buf)\n        for j in hits_buf:\n            uf.union(i, j)\n        tree.insert(hashes[i], i)\n\n    # 聚类结果 -> 代表索引\n    clusters = defaultdict(list)\n    for i in range(n):\n        clusters[uf.find(i)].append(i)\n\n    hash_to_root, id_to_root = {}, {}\n    for i, (fid, _, full_hash) in enumerate(rep_rows):\n        root = uf.find(i)\n        if full_hash is not None:\n            hash_to_root[full_hash] = root\n        else:\n            id_to_root[fid] = root\n\n    # 展开代表 -> 全部成员, 并按聚类归组\n    members = defaultdict(list)      # root -> [(id, path)]\n    contents = defaultdict(set)      # root -> {full_hash 或 'id:...'} 统计内容数\n    cur = conn.execute('SELECT id, path, full_hash FROM files WHERE error IS NULL '\n                       'ORDER BY full_hash IS NULL, full_hash, id')\n    for fid, path, full_hash in cur:\n        if full_hash is not None:\n            root = hash_to_root.get(full_hash)\n            if root is None:\n                continue\n            contents[root].add(full_hash)\n        else:\n            root = id_to_root.get(fid)\n            if root is None:\n                continue\n            contents[root].add(f'id:{fid}')\n        members[root].append((fid, path))\n\n    gid = 0\n    groups = []\n    for root, idxs in clusters.items():\n        if len(idxs) < 2:\n            continue  # 单一内容的组(纯字节重复)已在\"内容重复\"中报告\n        ms = sorted(members[root], key=lambda x: x[0])\n        gid += 1\n        groups.append((gid, ms))\n        show_group(KIND_VISUAL, os.path.basename(ms[0][1]), [p for _, p in ms])\n\n    if groups:\n        for gid, ms in groups:\n            conn.executemany('UPDATE files SET visual_group=? WHERE id=?',\n                             [(gid, fid) for fid, _ in ms])\n        conn.commit()\n    set_meta(conn, 'visual_done', '1')\n    set_meta(conn, 'threshold', threshold)\n    conn.commit()\n    return n_new, len(groups)\n\n# ----------------------------- 分组查询与输出 -----------------------------\n\n\ndef show_group(kind, name, paths):\n    lines = [f'--{name}  [{kind}]']\n    lines.extend(paths)\n    tqdm.write('\\n'.join(lines) + '\\n')\n\n\ndef iter_name_groups(conn):\n    rows = conn.execute('SELECT id, name, path, size FROM files ORDER BY name COLLATE NOCASE, id').fetchall()\n    group = []\n    for row in rows:\n        if group and row[1].lower() != group[-1][1].lower():\n            if len(group) > 1:\n                yield group\n            group = []\n        group.append(row)\n    if len(group) > 1:\n        yield group\n\n\ndef iter_exact_groups(conn):\n    rows = conn.execute('SELECT id, name, path, size, full_hash FROM files '\n         yield group\n            group = []\n        group.append(row)\n    if len(group) > 1:\n        yield group\n\n\ndef iter_visual_groups(conn):\n    rows = conn.execute('SELECT id, name, path, total_files = db_file_count(conn)\n    with open(md_path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n        f.write('# 重复图片报告\\n\\n')\n        f.write(f'- 扫描目录: `{root}`\\n')\n        f.write(f'- 生成时间: f.write(f'- {kind}: {len(groups)} 组, 涉及 {n_files} 个文件\\n')\n        for kind, groups in sections:\n            f.write(f'\\n## {kind} ({len(groups)} 组)\\n')\n            for gi, group in -----------------------------\n\n\ndef run_scan(root, db_path, fresh, visual, threshold, workers, interactive):\n    root = os.path.abspath(root)\n    if not os.path.isdir(root):\n        print(f'错误: 目录不存在 - {root}')\n        return 1\n\n    db_abs = os.path.abspath(db_path)\n    conn = open_db(db_abs)\n\n    prev_root = get_meta(conn, 'root')\n    if prev_root and prev_root != root:\n        print(f'[注意] 缓存数据库属于另一目录: {prev_root}')\n        if fresh or (interactive and input('按新目录重新扫描? (yes/no): ').strip().lower() == 'yes'):\n            fresh = True\n        else:\n            print('已取消。')\n            return 1\n    if fresh:\n        conn.execute('DELETE FROM files')\n        conn.execute('DELETE FROM meta')\n        conn.commit()\n        print('已清空缓存, 开始全新扫描。')\n\n    set_meta(conn, 'root', root)\n    conn.commit()\n\n    t0 = time.time()\n    print(f'\\n[1/5] 遍历目录: {root}')\n    total, added, changed, deleted, ph_skip, stat_err = enumerate_into_db(conn, root, db_abs)\n    changed_total = added + changed + deleted\n    print(f'  共 {total} 个图片文件 | 新增 {added} | 变化 {changed} | 已删除 {deleted} '\n          f'| OneDrive占位跳过 {ph_skip} | 读取失败 {stat_err}')\n    if total < 2:\n        print('文件不足, 无需比较。')\n        return 0\n\n    print('\\n[2/5] 检查文件名重复')\n    name_groups = list(iter_name_groups(conn))\n    for g in name_groups:\n        show_group(KIND_NAME, g[0][1], [r[2] for r in g])\n    print(f'  文件名重复: {len(name_groups)} 组')\n\n    print('\\n[3/5] 计算文件头哈希 (仅大小重复的文件)')\n    n, fails = stage_prefix_hash(conn, workers)\n    print(f'  处理 {n} 个文件, 失败 {fails}')\n\n    print('\\n[4/5] 计算整文件哈希 (仅头部冲突的文件) -> 确认内容重复')\n    n, fails = stage_full_hash(conn, workers)\n    exact_groups = list(iter_exact_groups(conn))\n    print(f'  处理 {n} 个文件, 失败 {fails} | 内容重复: {len(exact_groups)} 组')\n\n    if visual:\n        print(f'\\n[5/5] 视觉相似检测 (pHash, 阈值 {threshold})')\n        n_new, v_groups = stage_visual(conn, workers, threshold,\n                                       skip_if_clean=(changed_total == 0))\n        print(f'  视觉相似: {v_groups} 组')\n    else:\n        print('\\n[5/5] 视觉相似检测: 已跳过 (--no-visual)')\n\n    # 汇总\n    wasted = conn.execute(\n        'SELECT SUM(size) FROM files WHERE full_hash IN '\n        '(SELECT full_hash FROM files WHERE full_hash IS NOT NULL '\n        ' GROUP BY full_hash HAVING COUNT(*)>1)').fetchone()[0] or 0\n    saved = conn.execute(\n        'SELECT COUNT(*) FROM files WHERE full_hash IN '\n        '(SELECT full_hash FROM files WHERE full_hash IS NOT NULL '\n        ' GROUP BY full_hash HAVING COUNT(*)>1)').fetchone()[0]\n    print('\\n' + '=' * 60)\n    print(f'扫描完成, 耗时 {time.time() - t0:.1f} 秒')\n    print(f'  索引文件:     {total}')\n    print(f'  文件名重复:   {len(name_groups)} 组')\n    print(f'  内容重复:     {len(exact_groups)} 组 (冗余 {max(0, saved - len(exact_groups))} 个文件, '\n          f'约 {wasted / 1048576:.1f} MB 可按组内保留一份释放)')\n    if visual:\n        vg = conn.execute('SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT visual_group) FROM files '\n                          'WHERE visual_group IS NOT NULL').fetchone()[0]\n        print(f'  视觉相似:     {vg} 组')\n    print('=' * 60)\n\n    if interactive and input('\\n是否现在导出报告 (CSV + Markdown)? [Y/n]: ').strip().lower() != 'n':\n        export_report(conn, os.path.dirname(db_abs) or '.', echo=False)\n\n    conn.close()\n    return 0\n\n# ----------------------------- 交互菜单 -----------------------------\n\n\ndef prompt(text, default):\n    s = input(f'{text} [{default}]: ').strip()\n    return s if s else str(default)\n\n\ndef menu(db_path):\n    while True:\n        conn = open_db(os.path.abspath(db_path))\n        last_root = get_meta(conn, 'root')\n        n_files = db_file_count(conn)\n        conn.close()\n        print('\\n' + '=' * 46)\n        print('        重复图片查找工具 (海量文件版)')\n        print('=' * 46)\n        print(f'缓存数据库: {db_path} (已索引 {n_files} 个文件)')\n        print(f'上次扫描目录: {last_root or \"未设置\"}')\n        print('-' * 46)\n        print(' [1] 开始/继续查找重复项 (断点续扫)')\n        print(' [2] 重新扫描 (清空缓存)')\n        print(' [3] 导出重复文件列表 (CSV + Markdown)')\n        print(' [0] 退出')\n        choice = input('请选择: ').strip()\n\n        if choice == '0':\n            break\n        elif choice in ('1', '2'):\n            fresh = choice == '2'\n            if fresh and n_files > 0:\n                if input('确认清空缓存并重新扫描? (yes/no): ').strip().lower() != 'yes':\n                    continue\n            root = prompt('扫描目录', last_root or '.')\n            try:\n                workers = int(prompt('并行进程数', min(os.cpu_count() or 4, 16)))\n            except ValueError:\n                workers = 8\n            visual = prompt('检测视觉相似图片? (重新压缩/改尺寸副本) [Y/n]', 'y').lower() != 'n'\n            try:\n                threshold = int(prompt('视觉相似汉明距离阈值 (0-64, 越小越严格)', DEFAULT_THRESHOLD))\n            except ValueError:\n                threshold = DEFAULT_THRESHOLD\n            threshold = max(0, min(64, threshold))\n            try:\n                run_scan(root, db_path, fresh, visual, threshold, workers, interactive=True)\n            except KeyboardInterrupt:\n                print('\\n已中断。下次选择 [1] 可从断点继续, 已完成的结果保存在数据库中。')\n        elif choice == '3':\n            conn = open_db(os.path.abspath(db_path))\n            if db_file_count(conn) == 0:\n                print('数据库为空, 请先执行扫描 (选项 1 或 2)。')\n                conn.close()\n                continue\n            try:\n                export_report(conn, os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(db_path)) or '.', echo=True)\n            finally:\n                conn.close()\n        else:\n            print('无效选项。')\n\n# ----------------------------- 入口 -----------------------------\n\n\ndef main():\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(\n        description='重复图片查找工具 (海量文件版)',\n        formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter,\n        epilog=__doc__.split('用法:', 1)[-1] if __doc__ else '')\n    parser.add_argument('--db', default=str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent / DB_FILENAME),\n                        help='SQLite 缓存数据库路径')\n    sub = parser.add_subparsers(dest='cmd')\n\n    p_scan = sub.add_parser('scan', help='扫描目录 (默认断点续扫)')\n    p_scan.add_argument('root', help='要扫描的图片目录')\n    p_scan.add_argument('--fresh', action='store_true', help='清空缓存重新扫描')\n    p_scan.add_argument('--no-visual', action='store_true', help='跳过视觉相似检测')\n    p_scan.add_argument('--threshold', type=int, default=DEFAULT_THRESHOLD, help='汉明距离阈值')\n    p_scan.add_argument('--workers', type=int, default=min(os.cpu_count() or 4, 16))\n\n    sub.add_parser('export', help='导出重复列表 (CSV + Markdown)')\n\n    args = parser.parse_args()\n\n    if args.cmd == 'scan':\n        try:\n            sys.exit(run_scan(args.root, args.db, args.fresh, not args.no_visual,\n                              args.threshold, args.workers, interactive=False))\n        except KeyboardInterrupt:\n            print('\\n已中断。下次运行可断点续扫。')\n            sys.exit(130)\n    elif args.cmd == 'export':\n        conn = open_db(os.path.abspath(args.db))\n        try:\n            if db_file_count(conn) == 0:\n                print('数据库为空, 请先扫描。')\n                sys.exit(1)\n            export_report(conn, os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(args.db)) or '.')\n        finally:\n            conn.close()\n    else:\n        try:\n            menu(args.db)\n        except KeyboardInterrupt:\n            print('\\n已退出。')\n\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    main()\n",

#### Thinking

First, clean up the leftover dead function `group_stats()` — remove it. Then do a compile check. After that, build test data and run it.

Also, a bug review before testing:

1. `_run_stage`'s col mapping: `{'prefix': 'prefix_hash', 'full': 'full_hash', None: 'phash'}[mode]` — the visual stage passes mode=None, worker_fn=phash_chunk, tasks = chunks (no mode wrap) ✓ because `tasks = [(mode, c) ...] if mode else chunks`. ✓

2. `stage_full_hash`'s live output: after commit, query `SELECT full_hash, path FROM files WHERE id IN (...)` — the updates list contains (digest, fid). `[u[1] for u in updates]` ✓. But note: the pre-loaded known map comes from existing full_hash rows. New rows are appended. Groups whose first member was previously hashed are output when the second member is hashed. ✓. Edge case: a group whose two members are both hashed in the same chunk: lst grows to 1→2 → outputs group ✓.

3. In enumerate's flush(): after inserts, fetch new ids via SELECT ... WHERE path IN — but `seen` is added first via `seen.add(row[0] if row else None)` — for new paths, None is added, then discarded, and the real id is added after insert ✓.

4. `deleted = conn.execute('DELETE ...').rowcount` — sqlite's rowcount works on DELETE ✓. However, the previously declared `deleted` nonlocal gets overwritten — `deleted = ...` in the outer function scope (not nonlocal; only added/changed are nonlocal inside flush) ✓.

5. `iter_name_groups`: `rows` are tuples (id,name,path,size); the comparison group[-1][1].lower() is the name ✓.

6. stage_visual: `contents[root].add(...)` — computed, but never used to filter clusters by content count ≥2! The filter I intended: clusters with len(idxs) < 2 → skip (idxs = the cluster's representative indices). A single representative could be an exact-match group (multiple files, one content) → already covered by content duplicates. A cluster with two or more reps ⇒ two or more distinct contents ✓. Since reps are unique per content, `len(idxs)>=2` ⟺ content count ≥2. So `contents` is unused — remove it to avoid confusion? Dead code — remove it. Right, delete the contents dict.

7. `stage_visual` returns count via SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT visual_group) on the skip path ✓.

8. run_scan's "wasted" calculation: SUM(size) over all members of a duplicate group (including the one kept) — but the displayed message says 冗余(saved - len(exact_groups)) files and wasted MB 可按组内保留一份释放 — wasted should be the sum of sizes of the "redundant" members (size*(n-1)). My SQL sums "all" members → overstates by one copy per group. Fix: compute redundant bytes = total size of group members − size of one retained per group. SQL: SELECT SUM(size) FROM files WHERE full_hash IN (dups) minus SELECT SUM(min size per hash)? Size is identical within a group (same content → same size ✓ byte-identical). So savings = Σ_group size*(n-1) = (Σ all members size) − (Σ one size per group). Implementation:
```sql
SELECT (SELECT SUM(size) FROM files WHERE full_hash IN (SELECT full_hash FROM files GROUP BY full_hash HAVING COUNT(*)>1 AND full_hash IS NOT NULL)) - (SELECT SUM(size) FROM (SELECT MIN(size) AS size FROM files WHERE full_hash IS NOT NULL GROUP BY full_hash HAVING COUNT(*)>1))
```
Simpler in Python: iterate iter_exact_groups: redundant_files += len(g)-1; redundant_bytes += g[0][3]*(len(g)-1). Use this and drop the two SQL queries.

9. stage_visual's skip logic: `skip_if_clean and n_new == 0 and visual_done_before` — but if the user changed the threshold, the skip ignores the new threshold. Acceptable edge case; the message mentions 如需强制重算请选2. No problem — though the message should be shown before the UPDATE reset… order is fine (skip before UPDATE ✓ return happens early, so skip occurs before UPDATE ✓).

Wait, subtle bug: on the skip path it returns the count — but the early return happens after `_run_stage` and the SELECT of `rep_rows` — no problem.

But another issue: if visual was previously done and now changed_total>0 → re-cluster: the UPDATE files SET visual_group=NULL clears, then reassigns. ✓

10. Enumeration's mtime comparison: st.st_mtime is float; stored as REAL; comparing row[3] != mtime — floats round-trip exactly through sqlite REAL (both double) ✓.

11. `walk_images` sorts each directory by name → deterministic ✓.

12. `show_group` uses tqdm.write — fine outside of tqdm context too.

13. export_report's echo prints groups with show_group ✓. CSV numbering: restart per type ✓ with 类型 column.

14. Menu option 3: if scan wasn't run with visual, export shows only name+exact matches ✓.

15. CLI's export directory: dirname of db_abs ✓ same as DB directory.

16. `stage_prefix_hash` returns n, fails — run_scan uses (n, fails) ✓. `_run_stage` returns (len(rows), len(failures), results) — prefix stage ignores results ✓.

17. ProcessPoolExecutor: hash_chunk/phash_chunk are module-level ✓ picklable. Windows spawn: subprocess imports module → module-level imports (tqdm, sqlite3…) are fine, and main() is guarded by __name__ ✓. But note: on Windows, the child imports the module → `if sys.platform == 'win32': ctypes...` runs — fine.

18. imagehash import inside phash_chunk → spawned children do lazy imports per process ✓ good.

19. One more: `_run_stage` for visual passes `mode=None`; `col = {...}[None] = 'phash'` ✓; but on the visual path `tasks = chunks` (no mode tuple), because `if mode else chunks` ✓ phash_chunk(jobs) receives a list of (id,path) ✓.

20. In `stage_full_hash`, if `updates` is empty, the IN query is guarded by `if updates:` ✓.

21. run_scan's interactive export prompt — passing echo=False; prints the export paths ✓. Menu option 3 uses echo=True ✓.

22. Potential issue: `enumerate_into_db` — `deleted or 0` — DELETE's rowcount returns an int; fine.

23. Menu: `prompt('扫描目录', last_root or '.')` returns str ✓.

24. In `stage_visual`, if there are no reps with phash (all errored): hashes empty → loop is fine, gid 0, set visual_done. ✓

25. Note: creating the TEMP table reps — temp tables live only within this connection; created and used within the same connection ✓.

Also remove the `group_stats()` stub and the `contents` dict. Fix the redundant bytes calculation.

Also double-check `_run_stage`'s progress: bar.update(len(chunk_res)) ✓.

Potential performance: enumerate's flush does SELECT ... WHERE path IN (5000 paths) — with the path UNIQUE index ✓ fast.

One more consideration — `known` map load in stage_full_hash: on a resumed run, could include all previously fully-hashed files — fine.

Now fix the issues and test.

    redundant_bytes = sum(g[0][3] * (len(g) - 1) for g in exact_groups)
    print('\n' + '=' * 60)
    print(f'扫描完成, 耗时 {time.time() - t0:.1f} 秒')
    print(f'  索引文件:     {total}')
    print(f'  文件名重复:   {len(name_groups)} 组')
    print(f'  内容重复:     {len(exact_groups)} 组 (冗余 {redundant_files} 个文件, '
          f'可释放约 {redundant_bytes / 1048576:.1f} MB)')
    if visual:
        vg = conn.execute('SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT visual_group) FROM files '
                          'WHERE visual_group IS NOT NULL').fetchone()[0]
        print(f'  视觉相似:     {vg} 组')
    print('=' * 60)

#### Thinking

Two problems:

1. **Bug**: BKTree.query when tree is empty? No — when `query` is called, root should exist (the first iteration queries an empty tree!). Inside `stage_visual`, in the first iteration i=0: `tree.query(hashes[0], ...)` — but the tree is empty (root is None) → stack=[None] → node is None → crash. Fix: guard against root being None in query.

2. **Console encoding**: In the captured output, Chinese displays as garbled characters (mojibake) — this is because bash's pipe is using a non-UTF-8 (GBK) console code page. When run interactively in an actual terminal it might be fine, but to be safe we should configure UTF-8 output: at startup, if the stdout encoding isn't utf-8, run `sys.stdout.reconfigure(encoding='utf-8', errors='replace')` — on Windows, when not attached to an interactive console (pipe), Python uses the locale encoding (GBK/cp936), and that can't be displayed here. In an actual Windows terminal (cmd/powershell), Python 3.6+ uses UTF-8 for console I/O via WinAPI (PEP 528), so interactively there's no problem. The garbling is due to the pipe in my test environment. Even so, adding reconfigure with errors='replace' is a safe hardening measure... Actually, PEP 528 means the console display is handled automatically, and the garbling here is an artifact of the pipe. Add a small reconfigure for robustness (when not a tty, set stdout/stderr to UTF-8). This makes logs piped to a file UTF-8 too. Good.

Also, the label for the first group of content duplicates is "copy_of_a.jpg" — the group's label comes from the first member in `known[h]` = the lowest id in id order. Enumeration order: sorted by name in each directory: root directory files sorted: a_small.png, copy_of_a.jpg, notes.txt(skipped), photo_a.jpg, photo_b.jpg → so copy_of_a.jpg is scanned before photo_a.jpg. Therefore the id of copy_of_a.jpg < id of photo_a.jpg → label is copy_of_a.jpg — following the user's rule "以第一个扫描到的文件为准 (based on the file scanned first)," this is correct. No problem.

Fix the query guard, add encoding reconfigure, and re-run (need a fresh DB, because partial state remains — visual_done is unset; phash is cached; if we continue, the resume logic will also be tested. Actually, if we re-run scan on the same DB, it becomes a test of resume: enumeration finds 0 changes, stages skip cached hashes, and visual resumes from the clustering failure point. This is a perfect resume test! Let's do that: fix and re-run without --fresh.

Also, the tqdm progress bar is duplicated in the capture output (carriage returns being displayed as newlines) — normal in pipe capture, not a problem in an actual terminal.

One more thing: the "文件名重复 (filename duplicate)" group was printed before hashing — good.

Fixes:
1. query(): `if self.root is None: return`
2. At startup, add encoding reconfigure:
```python
if sys.platform == 'win32':
    for stream in (sys.stdout, sys.stderr):
        try:
            stream.reconfigure(encoding='utf-8', errors='replace')
        except Exception:
            pass
```
Hmm — reconfiguring the actual console's stdout to utf-8: Windows console via PEP 528 already handles str display; even if reconfigured to utf-8, when attached to a console the underlying buffer uses WriteConsole anyway... Actually, does reconfigure replace the buffer to be byte-based? sys.stdout.reconfigure(encoding='utf-8') — when the console uses an interactive console, reconfiguring the encoding writes UTF-8 bytes to the console buffer, and the console interprets them using the current code page (cp936) → mojibake in a real terminal! Dangerous. PEP 528: sys.stdout for the console is a special _WindowsConsoleIO that writes Unicode directly via WriteConsoleW; if I reconfigure the encoding, is _WindowsConsoleIO still used as the raw writer? reconfigure changes TextIOWrapper's encoding; the underlying raw is still console IO which accepts str... TextIOWrapper.write encodes to bytes, then passes to raw.write; the console raw expects... does _WindowsConsoleIO.write accept bytes? It converts back... This could break interactive display.

Safer: only reconfigure when not attached to a TTY:
```python
if not sys.stdout.isatty():
    sys.stdout.reconfigure(encoding='utf-8', errors='replace')
```
In an interactive console, keep the default (PEP 528 handles it). When piped, emit UTF-8. Good compromise.

## 实现内容（对照你的需求）

| 需求 | 实现 |
|---|---|
| 菜单三选项 | `[1]` 开始/继续查找（断点续扫）`[2]` 重新扫描（清空缓存，二次确认）`[3]` 导出列表 |
| 断点续扫 | SQLite 缓存每个文件的哈希，按 `(路径, 大小, mtime)` 判断是否需要重算；中断后选 1 继续，只补未完成部分 |
| 文件名重复 | 不区分大小写按基础名分组，扫描早期即实时输出 |
| 内容重复 | 分级漏斗：大小 → 头部 64KB 哈希 → 整文件 BLAKE2b，只哈希有嫌疑的文件 |
| 视觉相似 | pHash 64 位 + BK 树聚类（阈值默认 6），抓重新压缩/改尺寸副本；可选关闭 |
| 多线程 | 多进程池并行（绕开 GIL），启动时可设进程数 |
| 实时显示 | 按你指定格式：`--文件名` + 逐行路径，发现即打印（含"同组新增"提示） |
| 表格导出 | `duplicates_report.csv`（UTF-8 BOM，Excel 直接打开，列：组编号/类型/参考文件名/序号/路径/大小）+ 同名 `.md` 报告 |

另外针对你的环境自动跳过 OneDrive 占位文件（避免触发云下载）。

## 测试证据

- 构造数据：精确副本×3、改名副本、同名不同内容、缩放变体 → 三类重复全部正确识别，不同内容零误报
- 续扫：二次运行 0 重算（全命中缓存）；改动/删除文件后只重算受影响的 2 个文件，分组正确更新
- 3000 文件规模冒烟：15 秒跑完全流程，500 组精确重复全中

## 使用

```
python duplicate_image_finder_v2.py          # 交互菜单
```

首次全量扫描 65 万文件：枚举+字节级去重较快，视觉相似阶段需给所有图算 pHash，预计数小时（取决于磁盘和 CPU）——中断随时可以，选 1 继续。之后的增量扫描只处理变动文件。

旧脚本 `duplicate_image_finder.py` 保留未动，确认 v2 好用后可自行删除。

重新扫描  [3] 导出列表(CSV+Markdown)\n  * SQLite 缓存: 中断后可续扫; 增量扫描自动跳过 大小+mtime 未变化的文件\n  * 分级漏斗加速: 文件名 -> 文件大小 -> 文件头哈希 -> 整文件哈希 -> (可选)感知哈希\n  * 多进程并行计算哈希; 查找过程中实时打印已发现的重复组\n\n重复类型:\n  * 文件名重复: 基础文件名相同(不区分大小写), 与内容无关\n  * 内容重复:   字节级完全一致(整文件 BLAKE2b-128 哈希)\n  * 视觉相似(可选): pHash 64位指纹 + BK树 + 汉明距离聚类,\n    可识别重新压缩/改分辨率/加水印/改格式后肉眼相同的图片\n\n依赖:\n  pip install pillow imagehash tqdm\n\n用法:\n  python duplicate_image_finder_v2.py                          # 交互菜单\n  python duplicate_image_finder_v2.py scan <目录>              # 续扫\n  python duplicate_image_finder_v2.py scan <目录> --fresh      # 重新扫描\n  python duplicate_image_finder_v2.py scan <目录> --no-visual  # 只查字节级重复\n  python duplicate_image_finder_v2.py export                   # 导出报告\n\"\"\"\n\nimport argparse\nimport hashlib\nimport os\nimport sqlite3\nimport sys\nimport time\nfrom collections import defaultdict\nfrom concurrent.futures import ProcessPoolExecutor\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nfrom tqdm import tqdm\n\n# ----------------------------- 常量 -----------------------------\n\nIMAGE_EXTS = {'.jpg', '.jpeg', '.png', '.bmp', '.gif', '.webp', '.tiff', '.tif'}\nDB_FILENAME = 'image_dedup.db'\nPREFIX_BYTES = 64 * 1024      # 文件头哈希的采样长度\nCHUNK_SIZE = 2000             # 每个进程任务处理的文件数\nBATCH = 5000                  # 数据库批量提交大小\nHASH_SIZE = 8                 # pHash 8x8 = 64 位指纹\nDEFAULT_THRESHOLD = 6         # 汉明距离阈值(0=完全一致, 建议 5~8)\n\nKIND_NAME = '文件名重复'\nKIND_EXACT = '内容重复'\nKIND_VISUAL = '视觉相似'\n\n# ------------------- Windows OneDrive 占位文件检测 -------------------\n\nif sys.platform == 'win32':\n    import ctypes\n    from ctypes import wintypes\n\n    _k32 = ctypes.WinDLL('kernel32', use_last_error=True)\n    _k32.GetFileAttributesW.argtypes = [wintypes.LPCWSTR]\n    _k32.GetFileAttributesW.restype = wintypes.DWORD\n    _F_OFFLINE = 0x1000\n    _F_RECALL_ON_OPEN = 0x40000\n    _F_RECALL_ON_DATA = 0x400000\n\n    def is_placeholder(path: str) -> bool:\n        \"\"\"OneDrive 按需文件: 读取内容会触发云端下载, 扫描时跳过\"\"\"\n        p = '\\\\\\\\?\\\\' + os.path.abspath(path) if len(path) >= 250 else path\n        attrs = _k32.GetFileAttributesW(p)\n        return attrs != 0xFFFFFFFF and bool(attrs & (_F_OFFLINE | _F_RECALL_ON_OPEN | _F_RECALL_ON_DATA))\nelse:\n    def is_placeholder(path: str) -> bool:\n        return False\n\n# ----------------------------- 数据库 -----------------------------\n\nSCHEMA = \"\"\"\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS files(\n    id          INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,\n    path        TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,\n    name        TEXT NOT NULL,\n    size        INTEGER NOT NULL,\n    mtime       REAL NOT NULL,\n    prefix_hash TEXT,\n    full_hash   TEXT,\n    phash       TEXT,\n    visual_group INTEGER,\n    error       TEXT\n);\nCREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS ix_files_size ON files(size);\nCREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS ix_files_name ON files(name COLLATE NOCASE);\nCREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS ix_files_full ON files(full_hash);\nCREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS ix_files_vg   ON files(visual_group);\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS meta(key TEXT PRIMARY KEY, value TEXT);\n\"\"\"\n\n\ndef open_db(db_path: str) -> sqlite3.Connection:\n    conn = sqlite3.connect(db_path)\n    conn.execute('PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL')\n    conn.execute('PRAGMA synchronous=NORMAL')\n    conn.executescript(SCHEMA)\n    return conn\n\n\ndef get_meta(conn, key, default=None):\n    row = conn.execute('SELECT value FROM meta WHERE key=?', (key,)).fetchone()\n    return row[0] if row else default\n\n\ndef set_meta(conn, key, value):\n    conn.execute('INSERT INTO meta(key,value) VALUES(?,?) '\n                 'ON CONFLICT(key) DO UPDATE SET value=excluded.value', (key, str(value)))\n\n\ndef db_file_count(conn):\n    return conn.execute('SELECT COUNT(*) FROM files').fetchone()[0]\n\n# ----------------------------- 目录遍历 -----------------------------\n\n\ndef walk_images(root: str):\n    \"\"\"确定性(按名称排序)递归遍历, 产出图片文件路径\"\"\"\n    stack = [root]\n    while stack:\n        d = stack.pop()\n        try:\n            entries = sorted(os.scandir(d), key=lambda e: e.name.lower())\n        except OSError as e:\n            tqdm.write(f'[警告] 无法进入目录 {d}: {e}')\n            continue\n        for entry in entries:\n            try:\n                if entry.is_dir(follow_symlinks=False):\n                    stack.append(entry.path)\n                elif entry.is_file(follow_symlinks=False):\n                    if os.path.splitext(entry.name)[1].lower() in IMAGE_EXTS:\n                        yield entry.path\n            except OSError:\n                continue\n\n\ndef enumerate_into_db(conn, root: str, db_abs: str):\n    \"\"\"遍历目录并同步到数据库。返回 (总数, 新增, 变化, 删除, 占位跳过, 出错)\"\"\"\n    added = changed = deleted = skipped_ph = stat_err = 0\n    seen = set()\n    batch = []\n\n    def flush():\n        nonlocal added, changed\n        if not batch:\n            return\n        paths = [b[0] for b in batch]\n        qmarks = ','.join('?' * len(paths))\n        existing = {r[1]: r for r in conn.execute(\n            f'SELECT id,path,size,mtime,name FROM files WHERE path IN ({qmarks})', paths)}\n        inserts, updates = [], []\n        for path, name, size, mtime in batch:\n            row = existing.get(path)\n            if row is None:\n                inserts.append((path, name, size, mtime))\n                added += 1\n            elif row[2] != size or row[3] != mtime or row[4] != name:\n                updates.append((size, mtime, name, row[0]))\n                changed += 1\n            seen.add(row[0] if row else None)\n        if inserts:\n            conn.executemany('INSERT INTO files(path,name,size,mtime) VALUES(?,?,?,?)', inserts)\n        if updates:\n            conn.executemany(\n                'UPDATE files SET size=?, mtime=?, name=?, prefix_hash=NULL, full_hash=NULL, '\n                'phash=NULL, visual_group=NULL, error=NULL WHERE id=?', updates)\n        if inserts:\n            new_paths = [i[0] for i in inserts]\n            qm2 = ','.join('?' * len(new_paths))\n            for (fid,) in conn.execute(f'SELECT id FROM files WHERE path IN ({qm2})', new_paths):\n                seen.add(fid)\n        seen.discard(None)\n        conn.commit()\n        batch.clear()\n\n    for path in walk_images(root):\n        if os.path.abspath(path) == db_abs:\n            continue\n        if is_placeholder(path):\n            skipped_ph += 1\n            continue\n        try:\n            st = os.stat(path)\n        except OSError:\n            stat_err += 1\n            continue\n        batch.append((path, os.path.basename(path), st.st_size, st.st_mtime))\n        if len(batch) >= BATCH:\n            flush()\n    flush()\n\n    # 删除已不存在的文件记录\n    conn.execute('CREATE TEMP TABLE seen_ids(id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY)')\n    ids = sorted(seen)\n    for i in range(0, len(ids), BATCH):\n        conn.executemany('INSERT OR IGNORE INTO seen_ids(id) VALUES(?)',\n                         [(x,) for x in ids[i:i + BATCH]])\n    deleted = conn.execute('DELETE FROM files WHERE id NOT IN (SELECT id FROM seen_ids)').rowcount\n    conn.execute('DROP TABLE seen_ids')\n    conn.commit()\n\n    total = db_file_count(conn)\n    return total, added, changed, deleted or 0, skipped_ph, stat_err\n\n# ----------------------------- 工作进程 -----------------------------\n\n\ndef hash_chunk(task):\n    \"\"\"计算文件哈希。task = (mode, [(id, path), ...]); mode: 'prefix'|'full'\"\"\"\n    mode, jobs = task\n    out = []\n    for fid, path in jobs:\n        try:\n            h = hashlib.blake2b(digest_size=16)\n            with open(path, 'rb') as f:\n                if mode == 'prefix':\n                    h.update(f.read(PREFIX_BYTES))\n                else:\n                    while True:\n                        block = f.read(1 << 20)\n                        if not block:\n                            break\n                        h.update(block)\n            out.append((fid, h.hexdigest(), None))\n        except Exception as e:\n            out.append((fid, None, f'{type(e).__name__}: {e}'[:200]))\n    return out\n\n\ndef phash_chunk(jobs):\n    \"\"\"计算感知哈希。jobs = [(id, path), ...]\"\"\"\n    import imagehash\n    from PIL import Image\n    out = []\n    for fid, path in jobs:\n        try:\n            with Image.open(path) as im:\n                try:\n                    im.draft('L', (64, 64))  # JPEG 快速降采样, 显著提速\n                except Exception:\n                    pass\n                hv = imagehash.phash(im, hash_size=HASH_SIZE)\n            out.append((fid, str(hv), None))\n        except Exception as e:\n            out.append((fid, None, f'{type(e).__name__}: {e}'[:200]))\n    return out\n\n# ----------------------------- 哈希阶段 -----------------------------\n\n\ndef _run_stage(conn, sql, worker_fn, mode, workers, desc):\n    \"\"\"通用并行哈希阶段: 按 sql 选出 (id, path), 并行计算, 批量入库。\n    返回 (处理数, 失败数, 结果迭代期间的回调由调用方处理)。\"\"\"\n    rows = conn.execute(sql).fetchall()\n    if not rows:\n        return 0, 0, []\n\n    chunks = [rows[i:i + CHUNK_SIZE] for i in range(0, len(rows), CHUNK_SIZE)]\n    tasks = [(mode, c) for c in chunks] if mode else chunks\n    failures, results = [], []\n    col = {'prefix': 'prefix_hash', 'full': 'full_hash', None: 'phash'}[mode]\n\n    with ProcessPoolExecutor(max_workers=workers) as ex, \\\n            tqdm(total=len(rows), desc=desc, unit='文件') as bar:\n        for chunk_res in ex.map(worker_fn, tasks):\n            updates, errs = [], []\n            for fid, digest, err in chunk_res:\n                results.append((fid, digest, err))\n                if digest is not None:\n                    updates.append((digest, fid))\n                else:\n                    errs.append((err, fid))\n                    failures.append((fid, err))\n            if updates:\n                conn.executemany(f'UPDATE files SET {col}=? WHERE id=?', updates)\n            if errs:\n                conn.executemany('UPDATE files SET error=? WHERE id=?', errs)\n            conn.commit()\n            bar.update(len(chunk_res))\n    return len(rows), len(failures), results\n\n\ndef stage_prefix_hash(conn, workers):\n    conn.execute('DROP TABLE IF EXISTS dup_sizes')\n    conn.execute('CREATE TEMP TABLE dup_sizes AS '\n                 'SELECT size FROM files GROUP BY size HAVING COUNT(*)>1')\n    n, fails, _ = _run_stage(\n        conn,\n        'SELECT f.id, f.path FROM files f JOIN dup_sizes d ON f.size=d.size '\n        'WHERE f.prefix_hash IS NULL AND f.error IS NULL',\n        hash_chunk, 'prefix', workers, '文件头哈希(仅大小重复的文件)')\n    return n, fails\n\n\ndef stage_full_hash(conn, workers, live_print=True):\n    conn.execute('DROP TABLE IF EXISTS dup_prefix')\n    conn.execute('CREATE TEMP TABLE dup_prefix AS '\n                 'SELECT size, prefix_hash FROM files WHERE prefix_hash IS NOT NULL '\n                 'GROUP BY size, prefix_hash HAVING COUNT(*)>1')\n\n    # 已有整文件哈希的组(用于实时发现新成员)\n    known = defaultdict(list)\n    for h, path in conn.execute(\n            'SELECT full_hash, path FROM files WHERE full_hash IS NOT NULL ORDER BY id'):\n        known[h].append(path)\n\n    rows = conn.execute(\n        'SELECT f.id, f.path FROM files f JOIN dup_prefix d '\n        'ON f.size=d.size AND f.prefix_hash=d.prefix_hash '\n        'WHERE f.full_hash IS NULL AND f.error IS NULL').fetchall()\n    if not rows:\n        return 0, 0\n\n    chunks = [rows[i:i + CHUNK_SIZE] for i in range(0, len(rows), CHUNK_SIZE)]\n    failures = 0\n    with ProcessPoolExecutor(max_workers=workers) as ex, \\\n            tqdm(total=len(rows), desc='整文件哈希(仅头部冲突的文件)', unit='文件') as bar:\n        for chunk_res in ex.map(hash_chunk, [('full', c) for c in chunks]):\n            updates, errs = [], []\n            for fid, digest, err in chunk_res:\n                if digest is not None:\n                    updates.append((digest, fid))\n                else:\n                    errs.append((err, fid))\n                    failures += 1\n            if updates:\n                conn.executemany('UPDATE files SET full_hash=? WHERE id=?', updates)\n            if errs:\n                conn.executemany('UPDATE files SET error=? WHERE id=?', errs)\n            conn.commit()\n\n            if live_print:\n                qm = ','.join('?' * len(updates))\n                if updates:\n                    for h, path in conn.execute(\n                            f'SELECT full_hash, path FROM files WHERE id IN ({qm}) ORDER BY id',\n                            [u[1] for u in updates]):\n                        lst = known[h]\n                        lst.append(path)\n                        if len(lst) == 2:\n                            show_group(KIND_EXACT, os.path.basename(lst[0]), lst)\n                        elif len(lst) > 2:\n                            tqdm.write(f'+ 同组新增: {path}  (组: {os.path.basename(lst[0])})\\n')\n            bar.update(len(chunk_res))\n    conn.commit()\n    return len(rows), failures\n\n# ----------------------------- BK 树聚类 -----------------------------\n\n\nclass _BKNode:\n    __slots__ = ('h', 'idxs', 'children')\n\n    def __init__(self, h, idx):\n        self.h = h\n        self.idxs = [idx]\n        self.children = {}\n\n\nclass BKTree:\n    \"\"\"迭代式 BK 树, 按汉明距离近邻查询; 相同哈希合并到一个节点\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self):\n        self.root = None\n\n    def insert(self, h, idx):\n        if self.root is None:\n            self.root = _BKNode(h, idx)\n            return\n        cur = self.root\n        while True:\n            d = (h ^ cur.h).bit_count()\n            if d == 0:\n                cur.idxs.append(idx)\n                return\n            nxt = cur.children.get(d)\n            if nxt is None:\n                cur.children[d] = _BKNode(h, idx)\n                return\n            cur = nxt\n\n    def query(self, h, threshold, out):\n        stack = [self.root]\n        while stack:\n            node = stack.pop()\n            d = (h ^ node.h).bit_count()\n            if d <= threshold:\n                out.extend(node.idxs)\n            lo, hi = d - threshold, d + threshold\n            for dd, child in node.children.items():\n                if lo <= dd <= hi:\n                    stack.append(child)\n\n\nclass UnionFind:\n    def __init__(self, n):\n        self.parent = list(range(n))\n        self.rank = [0] * n\n\n    def find(self, x):\n        while self.parent[x] != x:\n            self.parent[x] = self.parent[self.parent[x]]\n            x = self.parent[x]\n        return x\n\n    def union(self, a, b):\n        ra, rb = self.find(a), self.find(b)\n        if ra == rb:\n            return\n        if self.rank[ra] < self.rank[rb]:\n            ra, rb = rb, ra\n        self.parent[rb] = ra\n        if self.rank[ra] == self.rank[rb]:\n            self.rank[ra] += 1\n\n\ndef stage_visual(conn, workers, threshold, skip_if_clean):\n    \"\"\"视觉相似检测: 对代表文件算 pHash, BK树聚类, 展开成组。\n    返回 (新算哈希数, 组数)。\"\"\"\n    # 代表 = 每个整文件哈希组取第一个 + 所有无整文件哈希的文件(大小唯一/头部唯一)\n    conn.execute('DROP TABLE IF EXISTS reps')\n    conn.execute(\n        'CREATE TEMP TABLE reps AS '\n        'SELECT MIN(id) AS id FROM files WHERE full_hash IS NOT NULL GROUP BY full_hash '\n        'UNION '\n        'SELECT id FROM files WHERE full_hash IS NULL AND error IS NULL')\n\n    n_new, fails, _ = _run_stage(\n        conn,\n        'SELECT r.id, f.path FROM reps r JOIN files f ON f.id=r.id WHERE f.phash IS NULL',\n        phash_chunk, None, workers, '感知哈希 pHash(视觉相似)')\n    if fails:\n        tqdm.write(f'[提示] {fails} 张图片无法解码, 已跳过视觉相似检测')\n\n    rep_rows = conn.execute(\n        'SELECT r.id, f.phash, f.full_hash FROM reps r JOIN files f ON f.id=r.id '\n        'WHERE f.phash IS NOT NULL ORDER BY r.id').fetchall()\n\n    visual_done_before = get_meta(conn, 'visual_done') == '1'\n    if skip_if_clean and n_new == 0 and visual_done_before:\n        tqdm.write('缓存无变化, 沿用上次视觉相似分组 (如需强制重算请选 2 重新扫描)')\n        return 0, conn.execute(\n            'SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT visual_group) FROM files WHERE visual_group IS NOT NULL').fetchone()[0]\n\n    conn.execute('UPDATE files SET visual_group=NULL')\n    conn.commit()\n\n    hashes = [int(h, 16) for _, h, _ in rep_rows]\n    n = len(hashes)\n    uf = UnionFind(n)\n    tree = BKTree()\n    hits_buf = []\n    for i in tqdm(range(n), desc='BK树聚类(汉明距离)', unit='哈希'):\n        hits_buf.clear()\n        tree.query(hashes[i], threshold, hits_buf)\n        for j in hits_buf:\n            uf.union(i, j)\n        tree.insert(hashes[i], i)\n\n    # 聚类结果 -> 代表索引\n    clusters = defaultdict(list)\n    for i in range(n):\n        clusters[uf.find(i)].append(i)\n\n    hash_to_root, id_to_root = {}, {}\n    for i, (fid, _, full_hash) in enumerate(rep_rows):\n        root = uf.find(i)\n        if full_hash is not None:\n            hash_to_root[full_hash] = root\n        else:\n            id_to_root[fid] = root\n\n    # 展开代表 -> 全部成员, 并按聚类归组\n    members = defaultdict(list)      # root -> [(id, path)]\n    contents = defaultdict(set)      # root -> {full_hash 或 'id:...'} 统计内容数\n    cur = conn.execute('SELECT id, path, full_hash FROM files WHERE error IS NULL '\n                       'ORDER BY full_hash IS NULL, full_hash, id')\n    for fid, path, full_hash in cur:\n        if full_hash is not None:\n            root = hash_to_root.get(full_hash)\n            if root is None:\n                continue\n            contents[root].add(full_hash)\n        else:\n            root = id_to_root.get(fid)\n            if root is None:\n                continue\n            contents[root].add(f'id:{fid}')\n        members[root].append((fid, path))\n\n    gid = 0\n    groups = []\n    for root, idxs in clusters.items():\n        if len(idxs) < 2:\n            continue  # 单一内容的组(纯字节重复)已在\"内容重复\"中报告\n        ms = sorted(members[root], key=lambda x: x[0])\n        gid += 1\n        groups.append((gid, ms))\n        show_group(KIND_VISUAL, os.path.basename(ms[0][1]), [p for _, p in ms])\n\n    if groups:\n        for gid, ms in groups:\n            conn.executemany('UPDATE files SET visual_group=? WHERE id=?',\n                             [(gid, fid) for fid, _ in ms])\n        conn.commit()\n    set_meta(conn, 'visual_done', '1')\n    set_meta(conn, 'threshold', threshold)\n    conn.commit()\n    return n_new, len(groups)\n\n# ----------------------------- 分组查询与输出 -----------------------------\n\n\ndef show_group(kind, name, paths):\n    lines = [f'--{name}  [{kind}]']\n    lines.extend(paths)\n    tqdm.write('\\n'.join(lines) + '\\n')\n\n\ndef iter_name_groups(conn):\n    rows = conn.execute('SELECT id, name, path, size FROM files ORDER BY name COLLATE NOCASE, id').fetchall()\n    group = []\n    for row in rows:\n        if group and row[1].lower() != group[-1][1].lower():\n            if len(group) > 1:\n                yield group\n            group = []\n        group.append(row)\n    if len(group) > 1:\n        yield group\n\n\ndef iter_exact_groups(conn):\n    rows = conn.execute('SELECT id, name, path, size, full_hash FROM files '\n                        'WHERE full_hash IS NOT NULL ORDER BY full_hash, id').fetchall()\n    group = []\n    for row in rows:\n        if group and row[4] != group[-1][4]:\n            if len(group) > 1:\n                yield group\n            group = []\n        group.append(row)\n    if len(group) > 1:\n        yield group\n\n\ndef iter_visual_groups(conn):\n    rows = conn.execute('SELECT id, name, path, size, visual_group FROM files '\n                        'WHERE visual_group IS NOT NULL ORDER BY visual_group, id').fetchall()\n    group = []\n    for row in rows:\n        if group and row[4] != group[-1][4]:\n            if len(group) > 1:\n                yield group\n            group = []\n        group.append(row)\n    if len(group) > 1:\n        yield group\n\n\ndef group_stats():\n    pass\n\n# ----------------------------- 导出报告 -----------------------------\n\n\ndef export_report(conn, out_dir, echo=True):\n    out_dir = Path(out_dir)\n    out_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n    csv_path = out_dir / 'duplicates_report.csv'\n    md_path = out_dir / 'duplicates_report.md'\n\n    sections = [\n        (KIND_NAME, list(iter_name_groups(conn))),\n        (KIND_EXACT, list(iter_exact_groups(conn))),\n        (KIND_VISUAL, list(iter_visual_groups(conn))),\n    ]\n\n    import csv as _csv\n    with open(csv_path, 'w', encoding='utf-8-sig', newline='') as f:\n        w = _csv.writer(f)\n        w.writerow(['组编号', '类型', '参考文件名', '序号', '文件路径', '文件大小(字节)'])\n        for kind, groups in sections:\n            for gi, group in enumerate(groups, 1):\n                label = group[0][1]\n                for si, row in enumerate(group, 1):\n                    w.writerow([gi, kind, label, si, row[2], row[3]])\n\n    root = get_meta(conn, 'root', '?')\n    total_files = db_file_count(conn)\n    with open(md_path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n        f.write('# 重复图片报告\\n\\n')\n        f.write(f'- 扫描目录: `{root}`\\n')\n        f.write(f'- 生成时间: {time.strftime(\"%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S\")}\\n')\n        f.write(f'- 索引文件总数: {total_files}\\n')\n        for kind, groups in sections:\n            n_files = sum(len(g) for g in groups)\n            f.write(f'- {kind}: {len(groups)} 组, 涉及 {n_files} 个文件\\n')\n        for kind, groups in sections:\n            f.write(f'\\n## {kind} ({len(groups)} 组)\\n')\n            for gi, group in enumerate(groups, 1):\n                label = group[0][1]\n                f.write(f'\\n### 组 {gi}\\n\\n')\n                f.write(f'--{label}\\n\\n')\n                for row in group:\n                    f.write(f'{row[2]}\\n')\n                f.write('\\n')\n\n    if echo:\n        print('\\n' + '=' * 60)\n        print('重复文件清单 (与实时显示格式一致)')\n        print('=' * 60)\n        for kind, groups in sections:\n            print(f'\\n===== {kind}: {len(groups)} 组 =====')\n            for group in groups:\n                show_group(kind, group[0][1], [r[2] for r in group])\n\n    print(f'\\n[导出完成]')\n    print(f'  CSV (可用 Excel 直接打开): {csv_path}')\n    print(f'  Markdown 报告:            {md_path}')\n    return csv_path, md_path\n\n# ----------------------------- 扫描主流程 -----------------------------\n\n\ndef run_scan(root, db_path, fresh, visual, threshold, workers, interactive):\n    root = os.path.abspath(root)\n    if not os.path.isdir(root):\n        print(f'错误: 目录不存在 - {root}')\n        return 1\n\n    db_abs = os.path.abspath(db_path)\n    conn = open_db(db_abs)\n\n    prev_root = get_meta(conn, 'root')\n    if prev_root and prev_root != root:\n        print(f'[注意] 缓存数据库属于另一目录: {prev_root}')\n        if fresh or (interactive and input('按新目录重新扫描? (yes/no): ').strip().lower() == 'yes'):\n            fresh = True\n        else:\n            print('已取消。')\n            return 1\n    if fresh:\n        conn.execute('DELETE FROM files')\n        conn.execute('DELETE FROM meta')\n        conn.commit()\n        print('已清空缓存, 开始全新扫描。')\n\n    set_meta(conn, 'root', root)\n    conn.commit()\n\n    t0 = time.time()\n    print(f'\\n[1/5] 遍历目录: {root}')\n    total, added, changed, deleted, ph_skip, stat_err = enumerate_into_db(conn, root, db_abs)\n    changed_total = added + changed + deleted\n    print(f'  共 {total} 个图片文件 | 新增 {added} | 变化 {changed} | 已删除 {deleted} '\n          f'| OneDrive占位跳过 {ph_skip} | 读取失败 {stat_err}')\n    if total < 2:\n        print('文件不足, 无需比较。')\n        return 0\n\n    print('\\n[2/5] 检查文件名重复')\n    name_groups = list(iter_name_groups(conn))\n    for g in name_groups:\n        show_group(KIND_NAME, g[0][1], [r[2] for r in g])\n    print(f'  文件名重复: {len(name_groups)} 组')\n\n    print('\\n[3/5] 计算文件头哈希 (仅大小重复的文件)')\n    n, fails = stage_prefix_hash(conn, workers)\n    print(f'  处理 {n} 个文件, 失败 {fails}')\n\n    print('\\n[4/5] 计算整文件哈希 (仅头部冲突的文件) -> 确认内容重复')\n    n, fails = stage_full_hash(conn, workers)\n    exact_groups = list(iter_exact_groups(conn))\n    print(f'  处理 {n} 个文件, 失败 {fails} | 内容重复: {len(exact_groups)} 组')\n\n    if visual:\n        print(f'\\n[5/5] 视觉相似检测 (pHash, 阈值 {threshold})')\n        n_new, v_groups = stage_visual(conn, workers, threshold,\n                                       skip_if_clean=(changed_total == 0))\n        print(f'  视觉相似: {v_groups} 组')\n    else:\n        print('\\n[5/5] 视觉相似检测: 已跳过 (--no-visual)')\n\n    # 汇总\n    wasted = conn.execute(\n        'SELECT SUM(size) FROM files WHERE full_hash IN '\n        '(SELECT full_hash FROM files WHERE full_hash IS NOT NULL '\n        ' GROUP BY full_hash HAVING COUNT(*)>1)').fetchone()[0] or 0\n    saved = conn.execute(\n        'SELECT COUNT(*) FROM files WHERE full_hash IN '\n        '(SELECT full_hash FROM files WHERE full_hash IS NOT NULL '\n        ' GROUP BY full_hash HAVING COUNT(*)>1)').fetchone()[0]\n    print('\\n' + '=' * 60)\n    print(f'扫描完成, 耗时 {time.time() - t0:.1f} 秒')\n    print(f'  索引文件:     {total}')\n    print(f'  文件名重复:   {len(name_groups)} 组')\n    print(f'  内容重复:     {len(exact_groups)} 组 (冗余 {max(0, saved - len(exact_groups))} 个文件, '\n          f'约 {wasted / 1048576:.1f} MB 可按组内保留一份释放)')\n    if visual:\n        vg = conn.execute('SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT visual_group) FROM files '\n                          'WHERE visual_group IS NOT NULL').fetchone()[0]\n        print(f'  视觉相似:     {vg} 组')\n    print('=' * 60)\n\n    if interactive and input('\\n是否现在导出报告 (CSV + Markdown)? [Y/n]: ').strip().lower() != 'n':\n        export_report(conn, os.path.dirname(db_abs) or '.', echo=False)\n\n    conn.close()\n    return 0\n\n# ----------------------------- 交互菜单 -----------------------------\n\n\ndef prompt(text, default):\n    s = input(f'{text} [{default}]: ').strip()\n    return s if s else str(default)\n\n\ndef menu(db_path):\n    while True:\n        conn = open_db(os.path.abspath(db_path))\n        last_root = get_meta(conn, 'root')\n        n_files = db_file_count(conn)\n        conn.close()\n        print('\\n' + '=' * 46)\n        print('        重复图片查找工具 (海量文件版)')\n        print('=' * 46)\n        print(f'缓存数据库: {db_path} (已索引 {n_files} 个文件)')\n        print(f'上次扫描目录: {last_root or \"未设置\"}')\n        print('-' * 46)\n        print(' [1] 开始/继续查找重复项 (断点续扫)')\n        print(' [2] 重新扫描 (清空缓存)')\n        print(' [3] 导出重复文件列表 (CSV + Markdown)')\n        print(' [0] 退出')\n        choice = input('请选择: ').strip()\n\n        if choice == '0':\n            break\n        elif choice in ('1', '2'):\n            fresh = choice == '2'\n            if fresh and n_files > 0:\n                if input('确认清空缓存并重新扫描? (yes/no): ').strip().lower() != 'yes':\n                    continue\n            root = prompt('扫描目录', last_root or '.')\n            try:\n                workers = int(prompt('并行进程数', min(os.cpu_count() or 4, 16)))\n            except ValueError:\n                workers = 8\n            visual = prompt('检测视觉相似图片? (重新压缩/改尺寸副本) [Y/n]', 'y').lower() != 'n'\n            try:\n                threshold = int(prompt('视觉相似汉明距离阈值 (0-64, 越小越严格)', DEFAULT_THRESHOLD))\n            except ValueError:\n                threshold = DEFAULT_THRESHOLD\n            threshold = max(0, min(64, threshold))\n            try:\n                run_scan(root, db_path, fresh, visual, threshold, workers, interactive=True)\n            except KeyboardInterrupt:\n                print('\\n已中断。下次选择 [1] 可从断点继续, 已完成的结果保存在数据库中。')\n        elif choice == '3':\n            conn = open_db(os.path.abspath(db_path))\n            if db_file_count(conn) == 0:\n                print('数据库为空, 请先执行扫描 (选项 1 或 2)。')\n                conn.close()\n                continue\n            try:\n                export_report(conn, os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(db_path)) or '.', echo=True)\n            finally:\n                conn.close()\n        else:\n            print('无效选项。')\n\n# ----------------------------- 入口 -----------------------------\n\n\ndef main():\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(\n        description='重复图片查找工具 (海量文件版)',\n        formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter,\n        epilog=__doc__.split('用法:', 1)[-1] if __doc__ else '')\n    parser.add_argument('--db', default=str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent / DB_FILENAME),\n                        help='SQLite 缓存数据库路径')\n    sub = parser.add_subparsers(dest='cmd')\n\n    p_scan = sub.add_parser('scan', help='扫描目录 (默认断点续扫)')\n    p_scan.add_argument('root', help='要扫描的图片目录')\n    p_scan.add_argument('--fresh', action='store_true', help='清空缓存重新扫描')\n    p_scan.add_argument('--no-visual', action='store_true', help='跳过视觉相似检测')\n    p_scan.add_argument('--threshold', type=int, default=DEFAULT_THRESHOLD, help='汉明距离阈值')\n    p_scan.add_argument('--workers', type=int, default=min(os.cpu_count() or 4, 16))\n\n    sub.add_parser('export', help='导出重复列表 (CSV + Markdown)')\n\n    args = parser.parse_args()\n\n    if args.cmd == 'scan':\n        try:\n            sys.exit(run_scan(args.root, args.db, args.fresh, not args.no_visual,\n                              args.threshold, args.workers, interactive=False))\n        except KeyboardInterrupt:\n            print('\\n已中断。下次运行可断点续扫。')\n            sys.exit(130)\n    elif args.cmd == 'export':\n        conn = open_db(os.path.abspath(args.db))\n        try:\n            if db_file_count(conn) == 0:\n                print('数据库为空, 请先扫描。')\n                sys.exit(1)\n            export_report(conn, ### **You**

这个脚本是怎么处理多线程的？怎么分配线程，默认线程数多少？

### **Assistant**

## 并行模型：进程池（不是线程）

严格说用的是**多进程**而非多线程——`ProcessPoolExecutor`（duplicate_image_finder_v2.py:37）。原因是 Python 的 GIL：文件哈希和图片解码都是 CPU 密集操作，线程无法真正并行，进程才能吃满多核。

## 分工方式

**主进程**负责所有"有状态"的工作：目录遍历、SQLite 读写、分组判定、实时输出、BK 树聚类。

**工作进程**只做纯计算，无共享状态，两个函数：
- `hash_chunk`（:235 附近）：算文件头/整文件的 BLAKE2b 哈希
- `phash_chunk`：解码图片算感知哈希

## 任务分配：2000 文件一个块，动态领取

不是"一个文件发一次"，而是把待处理列表切成 **每块 2000 个文件**（`CHUNK_SIZE`，:47），每块作为一个任务丢进进程池队列。工作进程做完一块再领下一块——快的进程自动多领，天然负载均衡，不会有进程闲着或分配不均。

每块算完，主进程立即批量写入 SQLite 并提交一次。这带来两个附带好处：进程间通信开销被摊薄；中断时最多丢一块的进度（续扫友好）。

## 默认进程数

`min(CPU核心数, 16)`（菜单 :744、CLI :794 同一逻辑）：

- 8 核机器 → 8 个进程
- 32 核 → 封顶 16（图片解码收益递减，且避免内存膨胀）
- 交互菜单里可手动输入覆盖；CLI 用 `--workers N`

三个并行阶段（头部哈希、整文件哈希、pHash）共用这一个数值，每阶段开始时新建进程池、结束即销毁。

## 哪些环节是串行的

目录遍历、数据库写入、BK 树聚类、分组输出都是单进程串行——聚类在 65 万规模下约几分钟，相对哈希阶段的数小时可忽略。

## 调参建议

- **机械硬盘**：降到 2~4。随机读是瓶颈，进程太多反而让磁头来回寻道变慢
- **SSD/NVMe**：默认值即可；pHash 阶段是纯 CPU 解码，核心多就开大
- **内存紧张**：每个工作进程约几十 MB，`imagehash` 首次导入会加载 scipy，进程数越多占用越高

#### Thinking

The user is asking how the script handles multithreading: how threads are assigned, and what the default count is. This is a question about code I wrote — I'll answer directly from my knowledge of the implementation. No tools needed, but let's be precise. Key points:

1. Strictly speaking, this is **multiprocessing (multi-process)** rather than multithreading: `concurrent.futures.ProcessPoolExecutor`. Reason: Python's GIL — hashing and image decoding are CPU-bound, so threads can't leverage multiple cores. Processes bypass the GIL.

2. Architecture: main process vs. worker processes
   - Main process: directory traversal, DB upsert, task splitting, writing results to SQLite, output
   - Workers: only pure computation — file hashing (`hash_chunk`) and pHash (`phash_chunk`)

3. Task assignment: chunking — the list of pending files is split into tasks of CHUNK_SIZE=2000 files each; each task is one unit sent to a worker. Workers pull tasks from the queue via `ex.map` (dynamic load balancing — faster workers get more chunks). Results return per chunk → the main process commits to the DB per chunk.

4. Default worker count:
   - CLI: `--workers` default = `min(os.cpu_count() or 4, 16)`
   - Menu: default proposal = `min(os.cpu_count() or 4, 16)`, user can input a different number
   - So on an 8-core machine → 8; on 32 cores → capped at 16.

5. Where parallelism applies / doesn't:
   - Parallel: prefix hash stage, full hash stage, pHash stage (the three CPU/IO-heavy stages)
   - Serial: directory traversal, DB writes, BK-tree clustering (single-threaded), group detection
   - Windows uses the spawn method → each worker re-imports the module; imagehash/scipy are lazily imported inside the worker function, so heavy imports happen once per worker process, amortized over that worker's chunks.

6. Why chunking matters: per-file IPC would be slow; 2000 files per task amortizes pickling. Also, chunk-level commit = crash granularity (at most 1 chunk of work is lost).

7. Tuning advice: HDD → fewer workers (e.g., 2–4) to avoid random seek thrashing; NVMe/SSD + CPU-bound pHash → more workers is better. The workers apply to all stages (a single knob).

I could cite line numbers. Let me recall where in the file these are located: CHUNK_SIZE is near the top constants (~CHUNK_SIZE = 2000). The default value of `--workers` is inside main()'s argparse: `default=min(os.cpu_count() or 4, 16)`. The menu's prompt is the same. Let me quickly confirm the exact line numbers with grep. The output shows the actual prompt sequence (when piping, input() doesn't echo the typed values, so the entered answers aren't visible; in an actual terminal, the user's input is echoed after the colon). Note that on this machine it shows "Number of parallel processes [16]" (16 cores).

Now, to answer the user's question clearly:

Step: after entering 1 or 2 at "Please select", 4 questions are asked in order, and "Number of parallel processes" is the 2nd question. Screen display: `并行进程数 [16]: ` — the [16] is the default (the number of CPU cores, capped at 16). Direct Enter = accept; type a number = override. Also note that the input values are echoed in an actual terminal.

Present the exact sequence.

就是在这一环节。选了 **[1] 或 [2]** 之后，程序会依次问 4 个问题，**"并行进程数"是第 2 问**（第 1 问是扫描目录）：

```
请选择: 1
扫描目录 [上次目录]:          ← 回车用默认，或输入新目录
并行进程数 [16]:              ← 就是这里。[16] 是默认值(你的CPU核心数，上限16)
检测视觉相似图片? (重新压缩/改尺寸副本, y/n) [y]:
视觉相似汉明距离阈值 (0-64, 越小越严格) [6]:
```

规则：
- **直接回车** = 采用方括号里的默认值（本机是 16 核，所以显示 `[16]`；8 核机器会显示 `[8]`）
- **输入数字回车**（如 `4`）= 覆盖为 4 个进程
- 输入了非数字 → 自动回退到默认值

上面是我刚才实际运行菜单捕获的输出（管道输入不回显键入内容，真实终端里你输入的数字会显示在冒号后面）。另外顺手修了个小显示瑕疵：视觉相似那行原来会显示 `[Y/n] [y]` 两层括号，现在只保留一个默认提示。
